Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any electrical_engineering module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 991. The projective resolution has length at most 13 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to electrical_engineering theory, we decompose the problem into 991 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 13. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
